Title: Constitution en forme de bref de N. S. Père le Pape Innocent XII portant condamnation et prohibition du livre intitulé Explication des Maximes des Saints, etc., avec le Mandement de Mgr. l'Archevêque Duc de Cambrai, sa lettre au Pape, le procès-verbal de l'Assemblée provinciale des évêques de la province de Paris, et plusieurs autres pièces sur la même affaire, tant en latin qu'en françois
Creator: Innocent XII (the brief Cum alias, 12 March 1699); with pieces by Fénelon, the provincial assemblies of bishops and Louis XIV
Publisher: Paris; and sold at Brussels by Jean Baptiste de Leener, libraire sur le Marché au Bois
Publication Date: 1699
Rights: Public Domain (published 1699, more than 95 years ago; public domain by date under the project's rolling rule; the item's own rights statement reads 'http://creativecommons.org/publicdomain/mark/1.0/')
Language: fra
Source: Internet Archive item bub_gb_2QE9obfYbeMC (https://archive.org/details/bub_gb_2QE9obfYbeMC), the item's own bub_gb_2QE9obfYbeMC_djvu.txt full-text OCR derivative; contributing library per the item metadata: National Library of Naples; host metadata date: 1699. Fetched directly (curl/urllib, raw bytes) by the Library thread (Era VIII pass), 2026-10-23. sha256 of the raw fetched bytes: 0f11e3b3c8f0dfebd29d07e296a6324f9d3d77ea4269304cbff161a36b456130.
Provenance note: Header prepended by the Library thread; nothing else altered, no OCR error corrected, nothing retyped. National Library of Naples copy scanned by Google and contributed to the Internet Archive; good OCR, long s as f; repeated "Digitized by Google" lines are left in place.
Content note: The condemnation dossier of 1699: the brief Cum alias with its twenty-three censured propositions in Latin and French, a Latin 'Systema' of the propositions, Fénelon's Mandement of submission and his letter to the Pope, the procès-verbaux of the provincial assemblies of bishops accepting the brief, the King's Declaration and the archbishop of Paris's Mandement.
